# Milestone 4: experiments E1-E4

Generated by `scripts/m4_report.py` from `results/models` (1000 bootstrap replicates, seed 0; the reference models of the main campaign (`scripts/m2.py`) in E1 and E3); definitions in `docs/experiments.md`, section 3. Tables: 3 decimals, `n/a` = not available. Per-window tables are used only when they reproduce their JSON summary (decision M4-5: in milestone 3 the transfer evaluation overwrote the own `p1.<h>.parquet`).

## E1: one-step error relative to persistence (H5.1)

`ratio` = RMSE_a(model) / RMSE_a(persistence) of P0 on fold 0 of the driver split (seed 0); `low`, `high`: 95 % interval from 1000 driver-bootstrap replicates with common random numbers (the follower counts are drawn once per dataset and applied to every table). `seed_min`/`seed_max`: range over training seeds 0-2; `folds_min`/`folds_max`: range over the clean arms of folds 0-4 (probe events, SUBSET only); n/a with fewer than two runs.

## E1b: closed-loop spacing error from 1 s windows to the whole event

`<h>_rmse_s`: pooled spacing RMSE of P1 at the horizon (1 s windows from protocol `p1s`, 10 s and 30 s from `p1`, the whole event); `<h>_median_rmse_s`: median over the windows (over the events for the whole event); `whole_top5_share`: share of the whole-event summed squared spacing error carried by the five followers with the largest sums. The taus between the P0 ranking and the horizon rankings are point estimates (intervals of the P1 horizons: E3).

## E2: leakage inflation (H5.2)

Paired design (decision R2): both arms scored on the same probe events of folds 0-4, pooled; `inflation` = (clean - leaky) / clean (positive: the leak lowers the error); `mean_difference` (clean - leaky), its interval and `p_bootstrap` from `paired_difference` (1000 driver replicates, common random numbers), `p_wilcoxon` the signed-rank test on the per-driver differences, `p_holm` the Holm adjustment of `p_bootstrap` over the models of a dataset, subset and metric. Subset `all`: every probe event; `affected`: the probe events of drivers with at least one event in the leaky training part of the same fold.

## E3: rankings across protocols (H5.3, H5.4)

Point values of fold 0, seed 0 (lower is better; P2 and the synthetic P4 over the profiles and scenarios inside the speed range of the training data; P1 whole and P0 from `train.json`). Kendall's tau-b between every pair of rankings: `tau` and `p` on the point values, `low`/`high` the 95 % interval and `tau_boot` the mean over 1000 replicates (drivers for P0, P1 and the empirical P4; in-range profiles for P2 and the composite, speeds with an equilibrium for P3, in-range scenarios for the synthetic P4). A model without a value ranks last.

## E3b: composite variants and sensitivity

The composite of E3 (`v1`: mean normalised rank of the concave-growth error, the hysteresis error, the unstable fraction and the 100-vehicle collisions) has no realism input: a follower that keeps a large gap and never reacts is stable and never collides. The variants add the whole-event P1 spacing RMSE (`v1+p1`), the relative fundamental-diagram error of P2 (`fd_error_rel`; `v1+fd`) or both (`v2`, six inputs, equal weights). `borda v2` is the Borda count of v2 (sum of n - rank, normalised to [0, 1] with 0 = best); with average ranks on ties it is an affine function of the rank sum, so it equals v2 by construction. Leave one out: v2 without each of its inputs; weights: v2 with weight 2 on the unstable fraction, on the collisions and on both (the stability and collision inputs). `fd_error_rel` is pooled over the samples of every P2 profile (the 20 OpenACC windows and the pulse: P2 has no in-range version), compares the density bins with at least 200 samples on both sides (`n_FD_bins`, which differs between models), and has no per-profile version to resample: it is held at its point value in the replicates. Intervals (1000 replicates, seed 0): the rank interval of v2 and the interval of tau(P0, composite) resample the in-range profiles and the speeds with an equilibrium, as in E3, and the drivers of the whole-event P1 input with the draw of P0 (common random numbers); the v1 row reproduces the P0-composite row of E3. Ranks: 1 = best, average ranks on ties. `top3 changes`: entries into and exits from the top three (ties at the third position counted in) against v1 for the variants and against v2 for Borda, leave one out and weights. Pareto view: `on front` marks the models that no other model matches or beats in both the whole-event P1 error and composite v2 (both lower is better; the P1 error is one of the six inputs of v2).

## E3c: training ablations of the networks

Campaign `ablation` (decision M4-7): rollout-loss horizons of 15 s and 30 s (`*_loop_h15`, `*_loop_h30`), a 3 s input history (`*_hist30`), a four-fold width (`*_wide`) and the stability penalty evaluated in a neighbourhood of every equilibrium (`stability_mlp_nbhd`); fold 0, seed 0 of the driver split, same training budget as the reference entries. `unstable` = numerical P3 fraction at 0.05 m/s, `large` at 1 m/s; collisions of P2 over the in-range profiles, P4 synthetic in range. The 15 s and 30 s horizons do not exist on FollowNet-highD (15 s events).

## E3d: the composite with the human platoon reference (protocol p2h)

P2 scores the platoons against recorded followers behind the OpenACC leaders (17 of 19 windows with ACC followers); p2h repeats the protocol behind human-driven chains of NGSIM and TGSIM and the two manual AstaZero runs (docs/protocols.md). `acc_*`: in-range P2 inputs, `human_*`: the same inputs of p2h; `composite_v1h` and `composite_v2h`: the variants v1 and v2 built from the human reference (P1 whole-event error and P3 unstable fraction unchanged). Taus are point estimates between the rankings of the models of the main campaign.
